<!-- pair: MTP-000568 -->

[Electronic chapter marker 8.][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-002]

<!-- pair: MTP-000569 -->

Then that holder of the vajra
petitioned Vajradhara in these words:[^T08-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-003]

<!-- pair: MTP-000570 -->

“O, O, teacher, Vajrasattva,
what are the means
and intrinsic nature of the secret continuum?
Blessed One, speak to me.”[^T08-001][^T08-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-004][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-005][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-006]

<!-- pair: MTP-000571 -->

The teacher said: “Holder of the vajra,[^T08-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-007]

<!-- pair: MTP-000572 -->

the continuum of means of all buddhas—
from the distinction of essence within one Ground—[^T08-001][^T08-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-008][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-009][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-012]

<!-- pair: MTP-000573 -->

[Printed page 521.][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-010][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-011]

<!-- pair: MTP-000574 -->

[is] what is indicated and what indicates.[^T08-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-012][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-013][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-014]

<!-- pair: MTP-000575 -->

Since the single continuum that is indicated pervades all,
everything follows it.[^T08-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-012][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-015][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-016][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-017]

<!-- pair: MTP-000576 -->

Pervasion has two aspects:
pervasion by emptiness and pervasion by clarity.[^T08-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-012][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-018]

<!-- pair: MTP-000577 -->

Emptiness is one cause; because [it] lacks entities,
[it] transcends the phenomena of all entities.
[དངོས་ཏེ: construction unresolved], without appearance, with a single pervasion,
[it] becomes the Ground of all, one essence—
the all-pervading lord itself and awakening.[^T08-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-012][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-019][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-020][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-021][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-022][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-023][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-026][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-027]

<!-- pair: MTP-000578 -->

[With] non-arising, through the exhaustion of mindfulness,
primordial purity itself: from [its] distinction,
empty yet appearing, appearing yet empty,
[དངོས་ཏེ: construction unresolved], [it] is also absence of entities itself.[^T08-004][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-012][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-028][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-029][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-030][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-031][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-172]

<!-- pair: MTP-000579 -->

Because of exhaustion, [it] abides empty from the primordial beginning;
free from causes and conditions, [it is] the domain of emptiness.[^T08-005][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-012][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-032][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-033][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-034][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-035][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-036][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-039][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-040]

<!-- pair: MTP-000580 -->

[Printed page 522.][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-037][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-038]

<!-- pair: MTP-000581 -->

Pervasively spread, without entities, empty in essence,
stainless, unmixed and complete—
free from conceptualization, the intrinsic nature of emptiness;
because [it] pervades through emptiness, [it] is called “continuum.”[^T08-006][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-039][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-041][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-042][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-043][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-044][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-045][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-046][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-047][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-048]

<!-- pair: MTP-000582 -->

Clear, since intrinsic nature is unceasing,
illuminating, appearing and pervading all,
clear and warm and cool,
[it] holds the identity of lightness and motion.[^T08-007][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-039][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-049]

<!-- pair: MTP-000583 -->

Pervasion, primordial radiance and self-clarity;
that which liberates, the very seed of ripening—
because [it] turns away from emptiness, [it] pervades through clarity.[^T08-008][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-039][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-050][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-051]

<!-- pair: MTP-000584 -->

The simultaneous type and self-liberation,
self-arising; through clarity, [it] is connected with the body,
stainless and separately limpid.[^T08-009][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-039][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-052][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-053][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-054][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-055]

<!-- pair: MTP-000585 -->

In object-appearance, clear and free from grasping,
the knowing of the five gates is self-clear and complete.
Because clarity pervades the body, [it is] continuum.[^T08-010][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-039][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-056][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-057][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-060][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-061][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-062]

<!-- pair: MTP-000586 -->

[Printed page 523.][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-058][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-059]

<!-- pair: MTP-000587 -->

Through manifest realization of those,
the distinction is also called “means.”[^T08-011][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-060][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-063][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-064][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-065]

<!-- pair: MTP-000588 -->

According to differences in individuals’ conceptual minds,
the sequence is to be explained as follows:
self-liberation without exertion and effort,
and taking into practice with exertion.
Those without exertion are self-purified.[^T08-011][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-060][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-066][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-067][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-068]

<!-- pair: MTP-000589 -->

Through the three aspects of the key points,
take that very meaning into practice:
body and [missing member unresolved] and the object itself.[^T08-012][^CH08-G001823][^CH08-G001825][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-060][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-069][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-070][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-071][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-173]

<!-- pair: MTP-000590 -->

The key points of the body have three aspects:
the manner of a lion and the manner of an elephant,
and [what] is called “like a seer.”[^T08-013][^CH08-G001826][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-060][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-072][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-073][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-074][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-075][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-078]

<!-- pair: MTP-000591 -->

[Printed page 524.][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-076][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-077]

<!-- pair: MTP-000592 -->

As for the gate: that itself is undeluded,
and since the two that make clear do not vibrate,
space itself is also without vibration.[^T08-014][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-078][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-079][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-080][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-081][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-082]

<!-- pair: MTP-000593 -->

The key points of the object also have three aspects:
the pure basic space stays,
likewise the essence of awareness stays,
and all appearances stay.[^T08-015][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-078][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-083][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-084][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-085][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-086]

<!-- pair: MTP-000594 -->

The mental faculty, without movement, abides in its own place.
Ordinary mind, without movement, has mindfulness cleared.
Awareness does not move; the three embodiments are clear.[^T08-016][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-078][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-087][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-088][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-089][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-090][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-091]

<!-- pair: MTP-000595 -->

With the mental faculty and ordinary mind at ease, the flow of mindfulness ceases.
With body and speech at ease, [there is] freedom from suffering.
With awareness at ease, [it] arises as five embodiments.[^T08-017][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-078][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-092][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-093][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-094][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-097]

<!-- pair: MTP-000596 -->

[Printed page 525.][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-095][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-096]

<!-- pair: MTP-000597 -->

Through awareness arising, conditions for arising cease.
Through clear light arising, the subtle and coarse cease.
Through basic space and awareness arising, objects and ordinary mind cease.[^T08-018][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-097][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-098][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-099][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-100][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-101][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-102][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-103][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-104][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-105]

<!-- pair: MTP-000598 -->

Thus, the indicative continuum of means,
complete in one’s own continuum without being generated—
what indicates is the means;
abiding in continuity is called “continuum.”[^T08-019][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-097][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-106][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-107][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-108][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-109][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-110]

<!-- pair: MTP-000599 -->

Of the Tathāgata’s intrinsic nature,
clearly distinguish the core of the continuum.[^T08-020][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-097][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-111][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-112]

<!-- pair: MTP-000600 -->

By anything of cyclic existence and transcendence of sorrow,
this itself is not known:
endowed with the indivisible intrinsic nature of vajra,
of the single Ground of its own indication,
[its] intrinsic nature is one in emptiness itself;
[it is] the domain that generates complete buddhahood.[^T08-021][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-097][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-113][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-114][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-115][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-116][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-117][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-118][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-119][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-120][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-121][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-124][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-125]

<!-- pair: MTP-000601 -->

[Printed page 526.][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-122][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-123]

<!-- pair: MTP-000602 -->

The three cores of the continuum are named:
the continuum of intrinsic nature is as follows—
the Ground, core and quintessence themselves.[^T08-022][^CH08-G001861][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-124][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-126][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-127][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-128]

<!-- pair: MTP-000603 -->

The Ground: essence, intrinsic nature
and compassionate responsiveness pervade all and appear.
Endowed with the identity of three primordial knowings,
primordially, stains are pure from the beginning.
The sole [state] without gathering or separation
is the great secret of the buddhas.[^T08-001][^T08-023][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-124][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-129][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-130][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-131][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-132][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-133][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-134]

<!-- pair: MTP-000604 -->

Embodiment is the family in which the core is complete.
Embodiment’s continuum is pervaded by emptiness.
Clarity itself is also self-appearance.
Endowed with an intrinsic nature of non-appearing clarity,
the intrinsic nature in which the core is complete: [རྒྱད: unresolved].[^T08-024][^CH08-G001873][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-124][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-135][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-136][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-137][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-138][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-141][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-142]

<!-- pair: MTP-000605 -->

[Printed page 527.][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-139][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-140]

<!-- pair: MTP-000606 -->

All appearances are continuum;
everything connects and attaches to everything.
Intrinsic nature: within citta’s mandala,
the great core, complete without being generated.[^T08-025][^CH08-G001877][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-141][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-143][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-144][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-174]

<!-- pair: MTP-000607 -->

Therefore, through being drawn together, [it is] the core;
because [it] becomes the vitality of all,
[it] is clearly explained as the continuum of the core.[^T08-026][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-141][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-145][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-146][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-147]

<!-- pair: MTP-000608 -->

All buddhas, all gathered into one—
from all [their] essences, the quintessence extracted—
by gathering [their] core, [it] is called “quintessence.”
Drawn together as the quintessence of emptiness, clarity and awareness,
[it is] the great secret of bliss of all buddhas.[^T08-027][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-141][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-148][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-149][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-150][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-151][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-152][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-153]

<!-- pair: MTP-000609 -->

The Ground and core, endowed with flowers,
the essence of the self-awarenesses—
as “the quintessence of Great Perfection”
is also widely known from other tantras.[^T08-028][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-141][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-154][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-155][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-156][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-157][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-160][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-161]

<!-- pair: MTP-000610 -->

[Printed page 528.][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-158][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-159]

<!-- pair: MTP-000611 -->

An explanation of the secret continuum:
a transmission that can be held with certainty,
intrinsic nature, empty and all-pervading.[^T08-029][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-160][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-162]

<!-- pair: MTP-000612 -->

Further, the intrinsic nature of quintessence:
through gathering the core, [there are] three pure extracts,
pervaded by three clarities and knowing.[^T08-030][^CH08-G001896][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-160][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-163][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-164][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-165]

<!-- pair: MTP-000613 -->

Through three confidences and three non-conceptualities,
[one] attains certainty in unchanging quintessence.[^T08-031][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-160][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-166][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-167]

<!-- pair: MTP-000614 -->

Three appearances and three emptinesses:
quintessence, through being pure in intrinsic nature,
is definitively established as continuum.[^T08-032][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-160][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-168][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-169]

<!-- pair: MTP-000615 -->

Through the Ground, the first path, and result,
the continuum of intrinsic nature is certainly held.[^T08-033][^CH08-G001903][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-160][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-170][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-171][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-175][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-271]

<!-- pair: MTP-000616 -->

[Printed page 529.][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-270][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-272]

<!-- pair: MTP-000617 -->

Therefore, “continuum” is established through connection.
Since [it] is established through intrinsic nature’s settling,
[it] is called the continuum of intrinsic nature itself.[^T08-034][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-176][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-177][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-178][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-271]

<!-- pair: MTP-000618 -->

From the distinction of essence within one Ground,
“continuum of means” and “continuum of intrinsic nature”
appear there merely as designations,
yet essence is one Ground, intrinsic nature empty:
[they] are not different and do not change.[^T08-035][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-179][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-180][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-181][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-182][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-183][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-271]

<!-- pair: MTP-000619 -->

For example, if some person
has many names,
whichever [name] is used to call, [that person] appears accordingly;
unchanging meaning is likewise.”[^T08-036][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-184][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-185][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-186][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-187][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-271]

<!-- pair: MTP-000620 -->

Then the holder of the vajra
petitioned Vajradhara in these words:[^T08-037][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-188][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-271]

<!-- pair: MTP-000621 -->

“O, teacher, great heroic being,
the buddhas’ continuum of meaning—[^T08-037][^T08-038][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-189][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-190][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-271][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-274]

<!-- pair: MTP-000622 -->

[Printed page 530.][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-273][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-275]

<!-- pair: MTP-000623 -->

if means and intrinsic nature are like that,
then, of these secret continua,
what is the limit of their divisions?
The chief himself has not spoken of it before.
Tell me what the sequence is.”[^T08-037][^T08-038][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-191][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-192][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-193][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-274]

<!-- pair: MTP-000624 -->

Then that great Vajradhara,
from the deep absorption
that clearly distinguishes the continua, arose
and spoke these words to the one endowed with vajra:[^T08-037][^T08-038][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-194][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-274]

<!-- pair: MTP-000625 -->

“Ah! Holder of the vajra, listen.
That itself is to be explained:
the continuum of meaning and the continuum of phrases.
The meaning is the sequence taught before.[^T08-037][^T08-038][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-195][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-196][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-197][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-274]

<!-- pair: MTP-000626 -->

Clearly distinguish the sequence of the continuum of phrases:
conceptual elaborations are cut off; the meanings of cyclic existence
will be unravelled through the continuum.[^T08-039][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-198][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-199][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-200][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-274][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-297]

<!-- pair: MTP-000627 -->

Ocean and Sun,
Lion and King of Mountains;[^T08-040][^CH08-G001939][^CH08-G001940][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-201][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-202][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-274]

<!-- pair: MTP-000628 -->

[Printed page 531.][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-276][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-278]

<!-- pair: MTP-000629 -->

Wheel and Key,
Sword and [གསལ་ཤིང: title unresolved],
Molten Gold and Mother-and-Child Connection,
Mirror and Strung Pearls,
Snake Knot and Great Garuḍa,
Water Stream and Razor,
[རྒྱལ་པོས: title unresolved] and Storehouse.[^T08-040][^CH08-G001942][^CH08-G001943][^CH08-G001944][^CH08-G001945][^CH08-G001946][^CH08-G001947][^CH08-G001948][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-203][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-204][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-205][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-206][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-207][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-208][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-209][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-277]

<!-- pair: MTP-000630 -->

By distinguishing [them] in that way,
the continuum of phrases is made clear.
By distinguishing its meanings,
the essence itself will also be seen.
Holder of the vajra, hold [this] with certainty.[^T08-037][^T08-041][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-210][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-211][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-212][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-277][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-280]

<!-- pair: MTP-000631 -->

[Printed page 532.][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-279][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-281]

<!-- pair: MTP-000632 -->

If [you] know the three cores of the continuum,
it is like king, ministers and subjects—the three—assembling.[^T08-042][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-213][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-214][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-280]

<!-- pair: MTP-000633 -->

If [you] know the three pure extracts of the continuum,
it is like reaching the peaks of three mountains.[^T08-042][^CH08-G001957][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-215][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-216][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-280]

<!-- pair: MTP-000634 -->

If [you] know the three flowers of the continuum,
it is like three suns arising in space.[^T08-042][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-217][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-218][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-280]

<!-- pair: MTP-000635 -->

If [you] know the single summation of all continua,
it is like a fortress’s foundation stones being well set.[^T08-043][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-219][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-280]

<!-- pair: MTP-000636 -->

If [you] know the continuum that completes the depth of appearance,
it is like a fortress completed at its summit.[^T08-043][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-220][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-221][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-280]

<!-- pair: MTP-000637 -->

If [you] know the continuum [ཡེ་ཤེས་གསུམ་ཟློག: construction unresolved],
it is like cutting windows in the four directions.[^T08-044][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-222][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-280][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-298]

<!-- pair: MTP-000638 -->

If [you] know the two great limb continua,
it is like attaching [བ་གམ: architectural term unresolved] on the four faces.[^T08-045][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-223][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-280]

<!-- pair: MTP-000639 -->

If [you] know the continuum of yogic liberation,
it is like placing the king at the summit of the fortress.[^T08-046][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-224][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-225][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-280]

<!-- pair: MTP-000640 -->

If [you] know the continuum that is like the heart,[^T08-046][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-226][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-280][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-283]

<!-- pair: MTP-000641 -->

[Printed page 533.][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-282][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-284]

<!-- pair: MTP-000642 -->

it is like firmly fitting a door to it.[^T08-046][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-227][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-283]

<!-- pair: MTP-000643 -->

If [you] know the secret continuum [རང་གཞུང: construction unresolved],
it is like surrounding the perimeter with an iron wall.[^T08-047][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-228][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-229][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-283]

<!-- pair: MTP-000644 -->

If [you] know the continuum that is like a weapon,
it is like an armored guardian of the gate.[^T08-048][^CH08-G001977][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-230][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-231][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-283]

<!-- pair: MTP-000645 -->

Ah! Since all continua in that way
are supremely great wonders,
all their meanings are to be explained.[^T08-049][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-232][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-283]

<!-- pair: MTP-000646 -->

Further, the sequence of continua:
the two root continua
explain all phenomena as one.[^T08-049][^CH08-G001982][^CH08-G001983][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-233][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-234][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-283]

<!-- pair: MTP-000647 -->

The two explanatory continua, mother and child,
explain in the manner of leaves spreading.[^T08-049][^CH08-G001984][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-235][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-283]

<!-- pair: MTP-000648 -->

The two limb continua
explain as planets and stars arising in an ocean.[^T08-049][^CH08-G001986][^CH08-G001987][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-236][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-237][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-283]

<!-- pair: MTP-000649 -->

The two continua that make transmission and awareness clear
explain in the manner of flowers opening.[^T08-049][^T08-050][^CH08-G001988][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-238][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-283]

<!-- pair: MTP-000650 -->

The four continua of the pith-instruction class[^T08-049][^T08-051][^CH08-G001990][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-239][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-283][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-286]

<!-- pair: MTP-000651 -->

[Printed page 534.][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-285][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-287]

<!-- pair: MTP-000652 -->

explain in the manner of fruit ripening.[^T08-049][^T08-051][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-286]

<!-- pair: MTP-000653 -->

The three continua of enlightened intent abiding in itself
explain in the manner of eyes that see.[^T08-049][^T08-052][^CH08-G001993][^CH08-G001994][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-240][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-241][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-286]

<!-- pair: MTP-000654 -->

The two continua of entry and self-liberation
explain in the manner of the heart, the Ground of mindfulness.[^T08-049][^T08-053][^CH08-G001995][^CH08-G001996][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-242][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-243][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-286]

<!-- pair: MTP-000655 -->

The continuum of the learned one’s ritual
explains like [མེ་ལ་ཚེ་བྱེད་པའི་ཁྱེ: construction unresolved].[^T08-049][^T08-054][^CH08-G001997][^CH08-G001998][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-244][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-245][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-286][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-299]

<!-- pair: MTP-000656 -->

Thus, through all continua, all of them,
the meaning is explained most clearly.
Through distinguishing the essence of the summation,
[one] attains the intrinsic nature of all buddhas.
That is how [it] is to be put into words.”[^T08-055][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-246][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-247][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-286]

<!-- pair: MTP-000657 -->

Then the holders of the vajra,
through the secret continua being distinguished,
had an ordinary mind of joy, delight and satisfaction arise;
joining their palms to Vajradhara,
they earnestly petitioned in these words:[^T08-056][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-248][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-249][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-286][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-289]

<!-- pair: MTP-000658 -->

[Printed page 535.][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-288][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-290]

<!-- pair: MTP-000659 -->

“O, sixth Vajradhara,
if the sequence of all continua is like that,
also for karmic beings to come,
of [what] becomes one through summing all continua,
having drawn together the entire core,
how should [this] be taught to karmic beings?
Vajradhara, speak to me.”[^T08-057][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-250][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-251][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-252][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-253][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-254][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-289]

<!-- pair: MTP-000660 -->

Then the teacher solemnly spoke:[^T08-058][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-289]

<!-- pair: MTP-000661 -->

“All that is to be expressed, however [it is expressed],
though it amounts merely to enumeration,
draw the summary into profound meaning.[^T08-058][^CH08-G002020][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-255][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-256][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-289]

<!-- pair: MTP-000662 -->

Although the natural state cannot be encompassed by reflection,
[it] is three aspects of primordial knowing.[^T08-059][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-257][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-289]

<!-- pair: MTP-000663 -->

Although many aspects of the Ground of delusion are explained,
[it is] spontaneous presence and compassionate responsiveness.[^T08-059][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-258][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-289]

<!-- pair: MTP-000664 -->

Self-abiding embodiment, awareness and primordial knowing;
abiding: [ཐུགས: unresolved], the center of citta.
The path is four channels; wind causes movement.
The four gates that make [it] arise are eyes and so forth.[^T08-060][^CH08-G002026][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-259][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-260][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-261][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-289][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-291][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-292][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-296][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-300]

<!-- pair: MTP-000665 -->

Space, free from conditions, is gathered as the object.
Taking into practice is Cutting Through and Leapover.
The measure is the yoga of the four confidences itself.[^T08-061][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-262][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-263][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-264][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-291][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-296]

<!-- pair: MTP-000666 -->

Through joining the two, mother and child, in the intermediate state,
the very place of liberation is the beginning.”[^T08-062][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-265][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-291][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-296]

<!-- pair: MTP-000667 -->

Thus, from the precious tantra String of Pearls, the eighth chapter, teaching all continua gathered together.[^T08-063][^CH08-G002034][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-291][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-296][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-302]

<!-- pair: MTP-000668 -->

When awareness without ordinary mind had spoken in those words, awareness, the sharp vajra, and so forth; the Tathāgatas of the five families;[^T08-064][^CH08-G002037][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-266][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-291][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-294][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-296][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-301][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-303]

<!-- pair: MTP-000669 -->

[Printed page 537.][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-293][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-295]

<!-- pair: MTP-000670 -->

the wrathful ones of the three families; buddhas, Dharma and Saṅgha; deities and asuras; immeasurable ḍākinīs; and the world together with gandharvas rejoiced and openly praised what the Blessed One had said.[^T08-065][^CH08-G002042][^CH08-G002043][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-267][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-268][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-294][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-303]

<!-- pair: MTP-000671 -->

This, called the precious secret tantra String of Pearls, has been brought forth as a distinctive [work] among sixty-four groups of one hundred thousand tantras of intrinsic nature, Great Perfection. Complete.[^T08-066][^CH08-G002046][^CH08-G002047][^CH08-G002048][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-269][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-294][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-304]

<!-- pair: MTP-000672 -->

[Source annotation.] There is a commentary on the String of Pearls tantra called “Making Clear.”[^T08-067][^CH08-G002049][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-294][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-305]

<!-- pair: MTP-000673 -->

[Source annotation.] For the String of Pearls tantra: mother and child—three [grouping unresolved].[^T08-068][^CH08-G002050][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-294][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-305]

<!-- pair: MTP-000674 -->

Virtue! Virtue! Virtue![^T08-069][^CH08-G002051][^CH08-G002052][^CH08-G002053][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-294][^MTP-AUDIT-C08-306]

[^T08-001]: Location: golden-v1 S1771–S1778 and S1868, printed520 and526. The uncovered title forms རྡོ་རྗེ་འཛིན་པ / རྡོ་རྗེ་འཛིན are provisionally “holder of the vajra,” རྡོ་རྗེ་འཆང “Vajradhara,” སྟོན་པ “teacher,” བཅོམ་ལྡན་འདས “Bhagavān,” and སངས་རྒྱས “buddha.” S1773 has the shorter exact རྡོ་རྗེ་སེམས, provisionally taken as the name Vajrasattva; the missing དཔའ is not restored to the source. A literal vajra/ordinary-mind construction remains a possible reading of that shortened form. S1777 སྟོན་པས་གསུངས་པ་རྡོ་རྗེ་འཛིན is treated as teacher response followed by vocative, not as a reassigned speaker. Review the abbreviated name and these title boundaries; none establishes a general exception to ordinary mind or apprehending subject.

    **Phase D current disposition (2026-10-05; supersedes earlier terminology-status claims only):** Active row `རྡོ་རྗེ་འཆང་` → **Vajradhara**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. Active row `བཅོམ་ལྡན་འདས་` → **Blessed One**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. PD-001, MTP-000570: `བཅོམ་ལྡན་འདས`; current English **Blessed One** replaces “Bhagavān”. See [the whole-work review](../../FINAL-REVIEW.md#phase-d-review). Original source-audit evidence and approval history above are retained; unresolved constructions remain flagged.

[^T08-002]: Location: S1774–S1785, printed520–521. Canonical རྒྱུད remains “continuum” in the means/intrinsic-nature and indicated/indicating discussion; it is not automatically changed to tantra. Unlisted ཐབས is provisionally “means,” མཚོན་བྱ “what is indicated,” མཚོན་བྱེད “what indicates,” and ཁྱབ / ཁྱབ་པ “pervade/pervasion.” S1779 གཞི་གཅིག་ངོ་བོའི་ཁྱད་པར་ལས has a compressed relation: “within one Ground” is a provisional relation, and the predicate continues after the page marker at S1781. The single Ground is not multiplied into two substances. “The Ground” retains a consistent internal capital as a provisional presentation choice. Review that relation and the two modes of pervasion; the empty/clear instrumental forms preserve their canonical terms.

    **Phase D current disposition (2026-10-05; supersedes earlier terminology-status claims only):** Family review `ཐབས`: The means/discerning-knowing pair and the continuum-of-means exposition were compared across the whole work. Retain the source order, distinguish procedure where actually present, and exclude ཐབས་ཅིག together. No chapter preference is an exception and no new shared entry is activated. Active row `རྒྱུད་` → **Continuum**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. P1 approves tantra in titles/textual-genre contexts; continuum remains the non-literary default. Ambiguous naming constructions stay provisional. Active row `གཞི་` → **The Ground**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. P1 requires capital Ground only for technical uses; disputed support/foundation constructions are not settled by capitalization. See [the whole-work review](../../FINAL-REVIEW.md#phase-d-review). Original source-audit evidence and approval history above are retained; unresolved constructions remain flagged.

[^T08-008]: Location: S1806–S1808, printed522. ཡེ་གདངས retains radiance with primordial qualification; རང་གསལ is provisionally “self-clarity,” not an invented shortening of self-clear mindfulness. གྲོལ་བྱེད and སྨིན་པའི་ས་བོན are provisionally “that which liberates” and “seed of ripening,” retaining both descriptions without asserting whether they are separate subjects. S1810 རང་ཤར་གསལ་བས is provisionally divided “self-arising; through clarity,” while a closer modifier relation remains possible. ལུས / འབྲེལ / སོ་སོར་དངས are “body / connected / separately limpid,” provisionally.     དགེའོ`. Adzom wording: not fully transcribed or not securely resolved. Three successive blessings are separately visible after the notices. Each is retained as its own fixed closing-colophon object; none is conflated or dropped. English consequence: Retain all three blessings and their closing-colophon function. Evidence: [Adzom p. 537, image 547](https://github.com/Lotus-King-Translation/Mu-Tig-Phreng-Ba/blob/main/evidence/golden/08/00547.png); Row5 middle–right: three successive དགེའོ blessings, followed by terminal shads.
